Whether that is the whole of photo 06 is for the retake to say (its town, seed 1, was not opened at this close: the street-level view of seed 7's corner, `09-close_street_level_day.png` of 19df2d88, draws everything); still to read: a storey filled with pillar bays, and the eye put exactly at a roof's height by `eye_free`. Three of that close's eleven views hid their target again (`03-close_gate_d_day`: the eye at a balcony inside a crown; `05-close_plate_a_day`: a wall a metre from the eye; `07-close_mid_day`: towers between): the rig lets the eye stand where the point looked at cannot be seen. Again on 2026-11-12 in the set "plate" (cbdda94a): `03-plate_mid_day` (`[-47, -103, 25, 30, 40]`) shows a building's wall from near, the junction behind it. Read 2026-11-12 (`project/game/camera/camera_pull.gd`, `native/src/api/city_core_sight.cpp`, `data/game/camera.json`): the pull in front of a building is weighted by the zoom (`pull_full_half` 4.8 m, `pull_none_half` 19.2 m of half view): none past 19.2 m (the view at zoom 25 hidden by towers is the rig's rule there, as the reference's `pullWeight`), and between the two the eye is moved a SHARE of the way past what hides the target (`wanted * weight`), which is a place still behind it: at zoom 90 (half 6 m, weight 0.92) the eye stops 8 % short, before the wall it was to pass (`05-close_plate_a_day`). A share of a move past an obstacle is no place to stand: the rule to design (with the camera's feel played, `camera_feel_test`) is a pull that ends only where the target is seen, or does not move. Again on 2026-11-12 in the set "garden2": `00-garden2_hedge_a_day` (the eye over a roof, the hedge behind it) and `04-garden2_planter_day` (the eye inside a structure); closes when: from every view the player's rig allows, what stands in the state is drawn (a probe of drawn against state from street-level views; photos); spec: the render specification
